.. _class_NetwMultiplayer:

NetwMultiplayer
===============

**Inherits:** :godot:`MultiplayerAPIExtension`

The :godot:`MultiplayerAPI` implementation for a Networked session.

.. rst-class:: classref-introduction-group

Description
-----------

Use :ref:`Netw.of()<class_Netw_method_of>` to get it for a node. Its methods take the :godot:`RID` of an object such as a :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>` or a :ref:`NetwScene<class_NetwScene>`, and most game code uses those objects instead.

::

    var api := Netw.of(self)
    api.entity_despawn(NetwEntity.of(self).rid)
    api.scene_watch(Netw.scene(self).rid, peer_id)

\ Each method prefix matches the class that wraps it.

- ``scene_*``: :ref:`NetwScene<class_NetwScene>`\ 

- ``interest_*``: :ref:`NetwInterest<class_NetwInterest>`\ 

- ``session_*`` and ``stats_*``: :ref:`NetwSession<class_NetwSession>`\ 

- ``clock_*``: :ref:`NetwClock<class_NetwClock>`\ 

- ``predict_*``: :ref:`NetwPrediction<class_NetwPrediction>`\ 

- ``entity_*``, ``liveness_*``, and ``spawn_*``: :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>`\ 

- ``table_*``: :ref:`NetwTable<class_NetwTable>`\ 

- ``schema_*``: :ref:`NetwSchema<class_NetwSchema>`\ 

- ``lagcomp_*``: :ref:`NetwAction<class_NetwAction>`\ 

- ``peer_*``: :ref:`NetwPlayer<class_NetwPlayer>`\ 

- ``display_*``: :ref:`NetwDisplay<class_NetwDisplay>`\ 

- ``endpoint_*``: :ref:`NetwConnection<class_NetwConnection>`\ 

- ``transport_*``: :ref:`NetwTransport<class_NetwTransport>`\ 

- ``rpc_*`` and ``sync_*``: :ref:`NetwMemberConfig<class_NetwMemberConfig>`\ 

- ``service_*``: :ref:`NetwService<class_NetwService>`\ 

- ``database_*`` and ``persist_*``: :ref:`NetwDatabase<class_NetwDatabase>` and :ref:`NetwPersistence<class_NetwPersistence>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_authenticating:

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_packet:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**peer_packet**\ (\ id\: :godot:`int`, packet\: :godot:`PackedByteArray`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_packet>`

Same as :godot:`SceneMultiplayer.peer_packet <SceneMultiplayer#class_SceneMultiplayer_signal_peer_packet>`, for packets sent with :ref:`send_bytes()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_send_bytes>`. A packet whose first byte Networked reserves never arrives here. :ref:`send_bytes()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_send_bytes>` lists those bytes.

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **ClockParam**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE** = ``0``

:godot:`int` ticks per second. Must match on every peer, so it can only be set through :ref:`NetwClockConfig<class_NetwClockConfig>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_SYNC_MODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_SYNC_MODE** = ``1``

The :ref:`SyncMode<enum_NetwClock_SyncMode>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_PING_INTERVAL:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_PING_INTERVAL** = ``2``

:godot:`float` seconds between pings.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME** = ``3``

:godot:`int` most ticks run in one frame. Leftover time carries to the next frame.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_STALL_THRESHOLD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_STALL_THRESHOLD** = ``4``

:godot:`float` seconds. A longer frame is treated as a hitch and skipped.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_PANIC_SNAP_THRESHOLD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_PANIC_SNAP_THRESHOLD** = ``5``

:godot:`int` ticks. Past this difference, :ref:`NetwClock.SYNC_MODE_STRETCH<class_NetwClock_constant_SYNC_MODE_STRETCH>` corrects in one step.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_STRETCH_NUDGE_FACTOR:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_STRETCH_NUDGE_FACTOR** = ``6``

:godot:`float` share of the difference :ref:`NetwClock.SYNC_MODE_STRETCH<class_NetwClock_constant_SYNC_MODE_STRETCH>` corrects each frame.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET** = ``7``

:godot:`int` ticks the display runs behind the simulation, which gives interpolation room.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_LEAD_TICKS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_LEAD_TICKS** = ``8``

:godot:`float` ticks a client runs ahead of the server.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_MULTIPLIER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_MULTIPLIER** = ``9``

:godot:`float` weight of jitter in :ref:`clock_get_recommended_display_offset()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_recommended_display_offset>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_WINDOW:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_WINDOW** = ``10``

:godot:`int` number of recent pings averaged.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_STABILITY_THRESHOLD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_STABILITY_THRESHOLD** = ``11``

:godot:`float` seconds of jitter under which :ref:`clock_ingest_pong()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_ingest_pong>` reports ``is_stable``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_USE_PHYSICS_INTERPOLATION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_USE_PHYSICS_INTERPOLATION** = ``12``

:godot:`bool`. Whether :ref:`clock_get_tick_factor()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick_factor>` follows :godot:`Engine.get_physics_interpolation_fraction() <Engine#class_Engine_method_get_physics_interpolation_fraction>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_MANUAL_TICK:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_MANUAL_TICK** = ``14``

:godot:`bool`. When ``true``, ticks only run through :ref:`clock_step()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_step>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICK_FACTOR_OVERRIDE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_TICK_FACTOR_OVERRIDE** = ``15``

:godot:`float`. A fixed value for :ref:`clock_get_tick_factor()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick_factor>`. Negative turns it off.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_ROLE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_ROLE** = ``0``

:ref:`DisplayRole<enum_NetwDisplay_DisplayRole>`. How the entity is displayed. Usually :ref:`NetwDisplay.DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTO<class_NetwDisplay_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTO>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_LIVE_MODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_LIVE_MODE** = ``1``

:ref:`LiveMode<enum_NetwDisplay_LiveMode>`. How a predicted entity is displayed.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_LIVE_SMOOTH_TIME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_LIVE_SMOOTH_TIME** = ``2``

:godot:`float` seconds to smooth out a correction on a predicted entity.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_CHASE_GLIDE_TIME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_CHASE_GLIDE_TIME** = ``3``

:godot:`float` seconds :ref:`NetwDisplay.LIVE_MODE_CHASE<class_NetwDisplay_constant_LIVE_MODE_CHASE>` takes to smooth out a correction.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_TIMELINE_MODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_TIMELINE_MODE** = ``4``

:ref:`TimelineMode<enum_NetwDisplay_TimelineMode>`. How a remote entity is displayed.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_MAX_FORECAST_TICKS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_MAX_FORECAST_TICKS** = ``5``

:godot:`int` ticks :ref:`NetwDisplay.TIMELINE_MODE_FORECAST<class_NetwDisplay_constant_TIMELINE_MODE_FORECAST>` may guess ahead before it stops.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_SMART_DILATION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_SMART_DILATION** = ``6``

:godot:`bool`. Whether playback may slow down to cover late packets.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_LAG_ADAPT_RATE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_LAG_ADAPT_RATE** = ``8``

:godot:`float`. How fast the interpolation delay follows changes in latency. Higher reacts faster but is less smooth.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_FLOOR_SMOOTHING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_FLOOR_SMOOTHING** = ``10``

:godot:`float`. How much the smallest interpolation delay is smoothed over time.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_TRACE_INTERVAL:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_TRACE_INTERVAL** = ``12``

:godot:`int` ticks between debug traces, or ``0`` for none.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_VISUAL_ROOT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_VISUAL_ROOT** = ``13``

:godot:`NodePath`. The node the interpolated transform is written to. It may be set before the node exists.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_DIVERGENCE_EPSILON:

Property Descriptions
---------------------

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_allow_object_decoding:

.. rst-class:: classref-property

:godot:`bool` **allow_object_decoding** = ``false`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_allow_object_decoding>`

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- |void| **set_allow_object_decoding**\ (\ value\: :godot:`bool`\ )
- :godot:`bool` **is_object_decoding_allowed**\ (\ )

Same as :godot:`SceneMultiplayer.allow_object_decoding <SceneMultiplayer#class_SceneMultiplayer_property_allow_object_decoding>`, applied to :ref:`inner<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_inner>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_auth_callback:

.. rst-class:: classref-property

:godot:`Callable` **auth_callback** = ``Callable()`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_auth_callback>`

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- |void| **set_auth_callback**\ (\ value\: :godot:`Callable`\ )
- :godot:`Callable` **get_auth_callback**\ (\ )

Same as :godot:`SceneMultiplayer.auth_callback <SceneMultiplayer#class_SceneMultiplayer_property_auth_callback>`. It runs first on each link and receives what the other peer passed to :ref:`send_auth()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_send_auth>`. The join continues once the game calls :ref:`complete_auth()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_complete_auth>`, so the :ref:`Netw.configure_admission()<class_Netw_method_configure_admission>` handler runs after it. Set the same callback on every peer, or the join is refused with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNCONFIGURED <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNCONFIGURED>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_auth_timeout:

.. rst-class:: classref-property

:godot:`float` **auth_timeout** = ``3.0`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_auth_timeout>`

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- |void| **set_auth_timeout**\ (\ value\: :godot:`float`\ )
- :godot:`float` **get_auth_timeout**\ (\ )

Seconds a peer has from connecting until a link is admitted, or ``0`` for no limit. On the server it is a joining client's deadline unless :ref:`NetwAdmissionConfig.timeout()<class_NetwAdmissionConfig_method_timeout>` is declared, and the server tells the client that deadline. A client uses its own value until the server first replies, and on links to other clients. A join that passes its deadline is refused with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_TIMEOUT <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_TIMEOUT>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_connected_players:

.. rst-class:: classref-property

:godot:`Array`\[:ref:`NetwPlayer<class_NetwPlayer>`\] **connected_players** = ``[]`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_connected_players>`

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- :godot:`Array`\[:ref:`NetwPlayer<class_NetwPlayer>`\] **player_all**\ (\ )

Every player, sorted by peer id. Same as :ref:`players<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_players>`, except that a player who is leaving stays here a little longer.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_inner:

.. rst-class:: classref-property

:godot:`SceneMultiplayer` **inner** :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_inner>`

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- |void| **session_set_inner**\ (\ value\: :godot:`SceneMultiplayer`\ )
- :godot:`SceneMultiplayer` **session_get_inner**\ (\ )

The :godot:`SceneMultiplayer` this session wraps. It owns the :godot:`MultiplayerPeer`, the connection and authentication. Never ``null``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_is_local_client:

.. rst-class:: classref-property

:godot:`bool` **is_local_client** = ``false`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_is_local_client>`

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- :godot:`bool` **get_is_local_client**\ (\ )

``true`` when this peer has its own player, which is at :ref:`NetwSession.ROLE_CLIENT<class_NetwSession_constant_ROLE_CLIENT>` and :ref:`NetwSession.ROLE_LISTEN_SERVER<class_NetwSession_constant_ROLE_LISTEN_SERVER>`. It reads the same for every entity, so ask :ref:`NetwEntity.is_controlled_locally<class_NetwEntity_property_is_controlled_locally>` whether an entity is this player's.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- :godot:`bool` **get_is_online**\ (\ )

``true`` when :ref:`state<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_state>` is :ref:`NetwSession.STATE_ONLINE<class_NetwSession_constant_STATE_ONLINE>`.

----

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- :ref:`NetwPlayer<class_NetwPlayer>` **player_local**\ (\ )

The accepted :ref:`NetwPlayer<class_NetwPlayer>` for this peer, or ``null`` before its join is accepted.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- :godot:`int` **get_max_delta_packet_size**\ (\ )

Same as :godot:`SceneMultiplayer.max_delta_packet_size <SceneMultiplayer#class_SceneMultiplayer_property_max_delta_packet_size>`, applied to :ref:`inner<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_inner>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_max_sync_packet_size:

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- |void| **set_max_sync_packet_size**\ (\ value\: :godot:`int`\ )
- :godot:`int` **get_max_sync_packet_size**\ (\ )

Same as :godot:`SceneMultiplayer.max_sync_packet_size <SceneMultiplayer#class_SceneMultiplayer_property_max_sync_packet_size>`. Networked also keeps its own synchronization packets under this size.

----

.. rst-class:: classref-property

:godot:`Array`\[:ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>`\] **player_entities** = ``[]`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_player_entities>`

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- :godot:`Array`\[:ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>`\] **scene_player_entities_all**\ (\ )

Every entity spawned for a player with :ref:`Netw.spawn_player()<class_Netw_method_spawn_player>`, in every scene. Entities in different scenes can share a :ref:`NetwEntity.name<class_NetwEntity_property_name>`.

:godot:`bool` **refuse_new_connections** = ``false`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_refuse_new_connections>`

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_relay_sender:

.. rst-class:: classref-property

:godot:`int` **relay_sender** = ``0`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_relay_sender>`

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- :godot:`int` **rpc_get_relay_sender**\ (\ )

The peer that sent the RPC being run, like :godot:`MultiplayerAPI.get_remote_sender_id() <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_method_get_remote_sender_id>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_role:

.. rst-class:: classref-property

:ref:`Role<enum_NetwSession_Role>` **role** = ``0`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_role>`

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- :ref:`Role<enum_NetwSession_Role>` **session_get_role**\ (\ )

What this peer is in the session. :godot:`MultiplayerAPI.is_server() <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_method_is_server>` and :ref:`is_local_client<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_is_local_client>` are usually what a game wants.

- :ref:`NetwSession.ROLE_NONE<class_NetwSession_constant_ROLE_NONE>` no session

- :ref:`NetwSession.ROLE_CLIENT<class_NetwSession_constant_ROLE_CLIENT>` a client

- :ref:`NetwSession.ROLE_DEDICATED_SERVER<class_NetwSession_constant_ROLE_DEDICATED_SERVER>` a server with no player

- :ref:`NetwSession.ROLE_LISTEN_SERVER<class_NetwSession_constant_ROLE_LISTEN_SERVER>` a server that also plays

The node at :ref:`root_path<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_root_path>`, which is the root window or a :ref:`MultiplayerViewport<class_MultiplayerViewport>`. :ref:`session_set_root()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_set_root>` can change how it is found.

Method Descriptions
-------------------

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clear:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clear**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clear>`

Clears the replication state of the wrapped :ref:`inner<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_inner>` :godot:`SceneMultiplayer`. Players, services and entities are kept.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_auto_configure_offset:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_auto_configure_offset**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_auto_configure_offset>`

Measures the connection for five seconds, then sets :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET>` to the largest value :ref:`clock_get_recommended_display_offset()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_recommended_display_offset>` returned. Does nothing on the server.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_config:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwClockConfig<class_NetwClockConfig>` **clock_get_config**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_config>`

Returns a copy of the current clock settings. Changing the copy has no effect.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_monitor:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`float` **clock_get_monitor**\ (\ monitor\: :ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwClock_ClockMonitor>`\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_monitor>`

Returns one clock diagnostic value.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_param:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Variant` **clock_get_param**\ (\ param\: :ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>`\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_param>`

Returns the current value of one clock setting, as an :godot:`int`, :godot:`float` or :godot:`bool` depending on the setting.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_physics_factor:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`float` **clock_get_physics_factor**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_physics_factor>`

Physics frames per tick, unrounded. 60 physics ticks per second with a :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE>` of 30 returns ``2.0``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_recommended_display_offset:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`int` **clock_get_recommended_display_offset**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_recommended_display_offset>`

The display offset this connection needs, in ticks. It is the one-way latency plus the jitter times :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_MULTIPLIER<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_MULTIPLIER>`, rounded up. It is not applied. :ref:`clock_auto_configure_offset()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_auto_configure_offset>` applies it.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_simulation_behind_count:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`int` **clock_get_simulation_behind_count**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_simulation_behind_count>`

How many frames ended with a tick still waiting while :ref:`clock_set_gate()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_gate>` was on, which runs at most one tick per frame. It stays ``0`` while the gate is off. A count that keeps growing means this machine is too slow to keep up with :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`int` **clock_get_tick**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick>`

The current server-calibrated simulation tick.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick_factor:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`float` **clock_get_tick_factor**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick_factor>`

How far time has moved past the last tick, as a fraction of a tick. Use it to interpolate between ticks. :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_TICK_FACTOR_OVERRIDE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICK_FACTOR_OVERRIDE>` replaces it with a fixed value.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick_phase:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`float` **clock_get_tick_phase**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick_phase>`

How far into the current tick the clock is, from ``0.0`` to ``1.0``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_ingest_pong:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Dictionary` **clock_ingest_pong**\ (\ sample\: :godot:`float`, server_tick_at_pong\: :godot:`int`, server_tick_phase\: :godot:`float`, apply_lead\: :godot:`bool`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_ingest_pong>`

Adjusts the local tick toward the server's using one round-trip ``sample``, in seconds. :ref:`NetwClock.SYNC_MODE_SNAP<class_NetwClock_constant_SYNC_MODE_SNAP>` corrects at once, and :ref:`NetwClock.SYNC_MODE_STRETCH<class_NetwClock_constant_SYNC_MODE_STRETCH>` corrects a little each frame until the difference passes :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_PANIC_SNAP_THRESHOLD<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_PANIC_SNAP_THRESHOLD>`. Returns a :godot:`Dictionary` describing the adjustment.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴rtt_raw                     float   this sample's round trip, in seconds
    ┠╴rtt_avg                     float   the running average round trip
    ┠╴rtt_jitter                  float   the running jitter estimate
    ┠╴diff                        int     ticks the calibration moved this peer
    ┠╴tick                        int     the tick after calibrating
    ┠╴display_offset              float   the offset the display is running
    ┠╴recommended_display_offset  float   the offset this sample recommends
    ┠╴is_stable                   bool    whether jitter is under threshold
    ┖╴is_synchronized             bool    whether a calibration has ever landed

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_configured:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`bool` **clock_is_configured**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_configured>`

Returns ``true`` when a :ref:`NetwClockConfig<class_NetwClockConfig>` was applied. Without one the tick does not move.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_gated:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`bool` **clock_is_gated**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_gated>`

Returns ``true`` while :ref:`clock_set_gate()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_gate>` is on.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_simulating:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`bool` **clock_is_simulating**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_simulating>`

Returns ``true`` when physics may step this frame. Always ``true`` unless :ref:`clock_set_gate()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_gate>` is on.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_synchronized:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`bool` **clock_is_synchronized**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_synchronized>`

Returns ``true`` once the clock has synchronized with the server at least once.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_physics_step:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_physics_step**\ (\ delta\: :godot:`float`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_physics_step>`

Advances the clock by ``delta`` seconds and runs every tick that completes. The session calls it every physics frame unless :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_MANUAL_TICK<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_MANUAL_TICK>` is set. A frame longer than :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_STALL_THRESHOLD<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_STALL_THRESHOLD>` is skipped, and no frame runs more than :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME>` ticks.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_gate:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_set_gate**\ (\ armed\: :godot:`bool`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_gate>`

While on, physics steps only on frames that have a tick to run, so a tick covers the same physics time on every peer. A client runs at most one tick a frame while it is on, so its tick snaps to the server's when it falls further behind than the :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME>` setting allows a frame to catch up. :ref:`clock_is_simulating()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_simulating>` tells whether this frame steps. Calls nest, so it turns off when every ``true`` is matched by a ``false``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_param:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **clock_set_param**\ (\ param\: :ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>`, value\: :godot:`Variant`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_param>`

Changes one clock setting. The tickrate can only be set with :ref:`Netw.configure_clock()<class_Netw_method_configure_clock>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_set_synchronized**\ (\ value\: :godot:`bool`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_synchronized>`

Sets :ref:`clock_is_synchronized()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_synchronized>`. Set it to ``false`` to resynchronize from scratch on the next ping.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_step:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_step**\ (\ count\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_step>`

Runs ``count`` ticks immediately. It does not open a tick loop, so wrap it in :ref:`clock_tick_loop()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_tick_loop>` when predicted entities must run.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_tick_loop:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_tick_loop**\ (\ open\: :godot:`bool`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_tick_loop>`

Opens a tick loop when ``open`` is ``true``, and closes it otherwise. Predicted entities only run between the two.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_complete_auth:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **complete_auth**\ (\ id\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_complete_auth>`

Same as :godot:`SceneMultiplayer.complete_auth() <SceneMultiplayer#class_SceneMultiplayer_method_complete_auth>`, called from :ref:`auth_callback<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_auth_callback>` when the game's own check of ``id`` passes. The peer connects once its join is admitted. Returns :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNCONFIGURED <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNCONFIGURED>` when there is no :ref:`inner<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_inner>`.

Closes ``database`` once its pending operations finish. The promise resolves with an :godot:`@GlobalScope.Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>`, see :ref:`NetwDatabase.close()<class_NetwDatabase_method_close>`.

Removes ``slot`` and everything stored in it. Can be called before :ref:`database_open()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_open>`. Deleting the open slot fails with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_BUSY <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_BUSY>`. See :ref:`NetwDatabase.delete_slot()<class_NetwDatabase_method_delete_slot>` for the errors.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_open:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_open**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, slot\: :godot:`StringName`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_open>`

Opens ``slot``. Other operations fail until the promise resolves. Opening a different slot while one is open fails with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_BUSY <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_BUSY>`. See :ref:`NetwDatabase.open()<class_NetwDatabase_method_open>` for the errors.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_patch:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_patch**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, schema\: :godot:`RID`, id\: :godot:`StringName`, values\: :godot:`Dictionary`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_patch>`

Writes the properties in ``values`` to the record at ``id`` and keeps the rest. A missing record fails with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST>`. See :ref:`NetwDatabase.patch()<class_NetwDatabase_method_patch>` for the errors.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_read:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_read**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, schema\: :godot:`RID`, id\: :godot:`StringName`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_read>`

Reads the record at ``id``, see :ref:`NetwDatabase.read()<class_NetwDatabase_method_read>` for the result. ``schema`` is a sealed schema, such as one from :ref:`schema_find()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_schema_find>`.

A ``database`` that is not open fails the promise with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNCONFIGURED <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNCONFIGURED>`, an unsealed ``schema`` with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST>`, and an empty ``id`` with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_scan:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_scan**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, schema\: :godot:`RID`, filter\: :godot:`Dictionary`, cursor\: :godot:`String`, limit\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_scan>`

Reads up to ``limit`` records matching ``filter``, see :ref:`NetwDatabase.scan()<class_NetwDatabase_method_scan>` for the result. Pass an empty ``cursor`` to start, and the cursor of the previous page to continue.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_submit:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_submit**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, operations\: :godot:`Array`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_submit>`

Applies ``operations`` in order, see :ref:`NetwWriteBatch.submit()<class_NetwWriteBatch_method_submit>` for the result. Build ``operations`` with :ref:`NetwWriteBatch<class_NetwWriteBatch>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_write:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_write**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, schema\: :godot:`RID`, id\: :godot:`StringName`, values\: :godot:`Dictionary`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_write>`

Replaces the whole record at ``id``. ``values`` must hold every column ``schema`` declares and nothing else. See :ref:`NetwDatabase.write()<class_NetwDatabase_method_write>` for the errors.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_add:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **endpoint_add**\ (\ transport\: :godot:`RID`, address\: :godot:`String`, display_name\: :godot:`String` = ""\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_add>`

Adds a server at ``address`` reached through ``transport``, and emits :ref:`NetwConnection.endpoint_added<class_NetwConnection_signal_endpoint_added>`. Adding the same pair again returns the same :godot:`RID`. Returns an invalid :godot:`RID` when ``transport`` is unknown.

An empty ``address`` is stored as the address a join with it reaches, so an ENet row reads ``localhost`` and :ref:`endpoint_find()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_find>` finds it by either spelling.

::

    var endpoint := api.endpoint_add(
            api.transport_find(&"ENetMultiplayerPeer"),
            "203.0.113.42:21253")

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

Returns the endpoint for ``transport`` and ``address``, or an invalid :godot:`RID`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_add_property_set:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **entity_add_property_set**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, set\: :godot:`RID`, comp\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_add_property_set>`

Synchronizes the properties in ``set`` on the node ``comp`` of ``entity``. ``0`` is the entity's root node. Create ``set`` with :ref:`property_set_create()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_property_set_create>` and seal it with :ref:`property_set_seal()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_property_set_seal>` first.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the set was attached
    ┠╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  entity or set does not exist
    ┠╴ERR_INVALID_DATA    the set is not sealed
    ┠╴ERR_UNAVAILABLE     comp is not a node of this entity
    ┖╴ERR_UNCONFIGURED    property synchronization is unavailable

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

Gives ``entity`` a route received from another peer.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the route was assigned
    ┠╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  entity does not exist
    ┠╴ERR_INVALID_DATA    route is not positive

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_call:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **entity_call**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, comp\: :godot:`int`, method\: :godot:`StringName`, args\: :godot:`Array`, peer\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_call>`

Calls ``method`` as an RPC on the node ``comp`` of ``entity``. ``peer`` ``0`` sends it to every peer :ref:`rpc_call()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_call>` would. The method needs an ``@rpc`` annotation or a :ref:`Netw.configure_rpc()<class_Netw_method_configure_rpc>` declaration.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the call was sent
    ┠╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  the node or the method does not exist
    ┠╴ERR_UNAVAILABLE     the node is not ready on this peer yet
    ┖╴ERR_UNCONFIGURED    the method is not an RPC

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_arm:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **event_arm**\ (\ groups\: |bitfield|\[:ref:`EventGroup<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EventGroup>`\]\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_arm>`

Records every :ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` in ``groups`` for :ref:`event_drain()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_drain>` and stops recording the rest. ``0`` records nothing, which is the default. Recording never changes what the session does.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_drain:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`PackedInt64Array` **event_drain**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_drain>`

Returns and clears the rows recorded since the last call, oldest first. The session keeps the newest 4096 rows, so drain once a frame while armed.

::

    func _physics_process(_delta: float) -> void:
        var rows := api.event_drain()
        for at in range(0, rows.size(), NetwMultiplayer.EVENT_ROW_WIDTH):
            if rows[at] == NetwMultiplayer.EVENT_DESPAWNED:
                print("despawned route ", rows[at + 3])

\ A row is :ref:`EVENT_ROW_WIDTH<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_ROW_WIDTH>` values, and its :ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` kind says what ``a`` and ``b`` hold. The entity is the id of its :ref:`NetwEntity.rid<class_NetwEntity_property_rid>`, as :godot:`RID.get_id() <RID#class_RID_method_get_id>` returns it, and the route is what :ref:`entity_get_route()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_get_route>` returns on every peer.

.. code:: text

    PackedInt64Array
    ┖╴row
      ┠╴kind     int  an Event value
      ┠╴tick     int  the tick it happened on
      ┠╴entity   int  the entity's RID id, 0 when it concerns none
      ┠╴route    int  the entity's route, 0 when it concerns none
      ┠╴peer     int  the peer it concerns, 0 when none
      ┠╴verdict  int  an Error, OK unless the kind judges something
      ┖╴a, b     int  two values that depend on kind, 0 when unused
        ┠╴EVENT_STAGE_TRANSITION     a the stage left, b the stage entered
        ┠╴EVENT_CONTROL_CHANGED      a the peer before, b the peer after
        ┠╴EVENT_CONTROL_REQUESTED    peer is the requester
        ┠╴EVENT_INTEREST_ENTER,
        ┃ EVENT_INTEREST_EXIT,
        ┃ EVENT_OBSERVER_ENTERED,
        ┃ EVENT_OBSERVER_LEFT        a the layer's RID id, peer the observer
        ┠╴EVENT_PREDICT_CONSUME      a the queue depth, b the buffer
        ┠╴any other kind from
        ┃ EVENT_EPISODE_OPEN to
        ┃ EVENT_RECOVERY             a the prediction transition
        ┠╴EVENT_SYNC_ENCODE          a the bytes written
        ┠╴EVENT_GATHER               a the rows offered, b the rows sent
        ┠╴EVENT_APPLY                a the frames applied
        ┠╴EVENT_TABLE_COMMIT         a the rows committed
        ┠╴EVENT_DISPLAY_RECORD       a the tick of the sample
        ┠╴EVENT_DISPLAY_PUMP         a the display tick
        ┠╴EVENT_DATAGRAM_SENT        a the size, b the sequence
        ┠╴EVENT_DATAGRAM_RECEIVED    a the size, b the sequence or -1 when reliable
        ┠╴EVENT_DATAGRAM_MALFORMED   a the size
        ┖╴EVENT_ACK_ADVANCED         a the newest sequence acknowledged

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_flush_now:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **interest_flush_now**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_flush_now>`

Applies pending layer changes now, where they would otherwise apply at the end of the frame.

::

    layer.add_viewer(peer)
    api.interest_flush_now()
    assert(api.interest_admits(entity, peer))

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the changes were applied
    ┠╴ERR_INVALID_DATA    a driver callback did not return an Array
    ┖╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  a driver callback returned an unknown entity

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_layer_set_driver_callback:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **interest_layer_set_driver_callback**\ (\ layer\: :godot:`RID`, callback\: :godot:`Callable`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_layer_set_driver_callback>`

Makes ``callback`` decide which entities are in ``layer``. It runs once per frame with the layer's :godot:`RID` and returns the :godot:`RID` of every entity in it. An empty :godot:`Callable` removes it. :ref:`NetwInterestLayer.driver<class_NetwInterestLayer_property_driver>` is the same with objects, and setting one replaces the other.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

Calls ``callback`` as ``callback(visible, entity, peer_id)`` when an entity enters or leaves ``layer`` for a peer, where ``entity`` is an :godot:`RID`. :ref:`NetwInterestLayer.monitor<class_NetwInterestLayer_property_monitor>` is the same with objects, and setting one replaces the other.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_metrics:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Dictionary` **lagcomp_metrics**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_metrics>`

Returns lag compensation counters. Totals count from startup.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴entities          int         entities simulated this tick
    ┠╴timelines         int         rewindable entities recorded this tick
    ┠╴corrections       int         total corrections
    ┠╴installs          int         total server states written onto simulated entities
    ┠╴max_replay_depth  int         most ticks replayed at once
    ┠╴consumed          int         total inputs the server used
    ┠╴missing           int         total input ticks that never arrived
    ┠╴folded            int         physics frames merged into an existing input
    ┠╴joint             Dictionary  totals for bodies simulated together
    ┠╴pending_actions   int         actions waiting to run
    ┠╴effects_armed     int         effects waiting to be confirmed or denied
    ┖╴gate_fallbacks    int         actions that ran without all their state

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_rewind:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **lagcomp_rewind**\ (\ entities\: :godot:`Array`\[:godot:`RID`\], tick\: :godot:`int`, body\: :godot:`Callable`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_rewind>`

Moves every entity in ``entities`` back to where it was at ``tick``, calls ``body``, then moves them back. Entities with no history at ``tick`` are skipped. :ref:`Netw.rewind()<class_Netw_method_rewind>` does the same with :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>` values.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

Returns a copy of the state of ``entity`` at ``tick``. The record is empty when nothing was recorded.

::

    var past := api.lagcomp_sample(entity, tick)
    if past.has_value(&"position"):
        print(past.position)

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_peer_get_bucket:

:godot:`Variant` **peer_get_bucket**\ (\ peer\: :godot:`int`, bucket_type\: :godot:`Variant`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_peer_get_bucket>`

Returns an instance of ``bucket_type`` stored for ``peer``, created on first use. Use it to keep game data per peer.

::

    class Bucket extends RefCounted:
        var ready := false

    var bucket := api.peer_get_bucket(peer, Bucket) as Bucket

\ ``bucket_type`` must create a :godot:`RefCounted`. Returns ``null`` for an unknown peer. :ref:`peer_forget()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_peer_forget>` deletes the bucket.

.. rst-class:: classref-method

Disconnects ``peer_id``. When ``reason`` is not empty, that peer receives it first. Does nothing on a client.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_peer_link_stats:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Dictionary` **peer_link_stats**\ (\ peer\: :godot:`int`\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_peer_link_stats>`

Returns measured connection quality to ``peer``, and what this peer's byte budget sends it.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴loss           float   share of the last 32 packets lost
    ┠╴rtt            float   round trip time, in seconds
    ┠╴jitter         float   round trip variation, in seconds
    ┠╴reorders       int     packets that arrived out of order
    ┠╴duplicates     int     packets that arrived twice
    ┠╴mode           String  "good", or "bad" while its budget is cut
    ┠╴budget_bits    int     most bits one packet to this peer may use
    ┠╴budget_bytes   int     bytes it may be sent each tick
    ┠╴sent_bytes     int     bytes charged to it since it joined
    ┠╴debt_bytes     int     bytes it was sent past its budget
    ┖╴deferred_rows  int     rows left waiting by the last tick

\ Losing packets halves ``budget_bytes`` and ``budget_bits``. Each round trip without loss adds back an eighth of the configured budget, stopping an eighth short of the rate that lost packets for the next minute. While ``debt_bytes`` is above zero, volatile properties wait, and retained properties and RPCs still go.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

    ┠╴ERR_ALREADY_EXISTS  more than one node declared the same settings
    ┠╴ERR_UNAVAILABLE     the node that declared the settings left the tree
    ┖╴ERR_INVALID_DATA    the declared settings cannot be read

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_stepper_install:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **predict_stepper_install**\ (\ space\: :godot:`RID`, stepper\: :ref:`NetwPhysicsStepper<class_NetwPhysicsStepper>` = null\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_stepper_install>`

Lets ``stepper`` step the physics ``space`` in place of ``PhysicsServer3D.space_step``, for a physics server without it. A ``null`` ``stepper``, or one whose :ref:`NetwPhysicsStepper._can_step()<class_NetwPhysicsStepper_private_method__can_step>` returns ``false``, removes the stepper of ``space``.

Returns :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER>` when ``space`` is not valid. :ref:`NetwSession.install_physics_stepper()<class_NetwSession_method_install_physics_stepper>` does the same from a :godot:`World3D`.

Adds the schema's ``column`` to ``set`` and returns its index. Returns ``-1`` when the set is sealed, the column is unknown, or already added.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_property_set_create:

Creates an empty set of synchronized properties for ``schema``. Add properties with :ref:`property_set_add_column()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_property_set_add_column>`, then call :ref:`property_set_seal()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_property_set_seal>`. Returns an invalid :godot:`RID` when ``schema`` is unknown or has an array column.

:godot:`int` **property_set_get_wire_hash**\ (\ set\: :godot:`RID`\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_property_set_get_wire_hash>`

A hash of the properties of ``set`` and their order. Two peers with the same hash read each other's packets correctly. Returns ``0`` for an unknown set.

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **property_set_seal**\ (\ set\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_property_set_seal>`

Locks ``set`` so no more properties can be added.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the set was sealed
    ┖╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  the set does not exist

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_call:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **rpc_call**\ (\ callable\: :godot:`Callable`, args\: :godot:`Array`, peer\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_call>`

Calls ``callable`` with ``args`` as an RPC on ``peer``, or on every peer from :ref:`rpc_get_recipients()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_get_recipients>` when ``peer`` is ``0``. The node must belong to an entity, and the method must be an RPC. A :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>` in ``args`` arrives as the receiver's own :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_channel_register:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **rpc_channel_register**\ (\ channel\: :godot:`int`, handler\: :godot:`Callable`, defer_when_unknown\: :godot:`bool` = false\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_channel_register>`

Calls ``handler`` as ``handler(entity, payload, sender)`` for every packet on ``channel``, which must be between 100 and 254. When ``defer_when_unknown`` is ``true``, a packet for an entity that is not spawned yet waits for it.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_get_recipients:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`PackedInt32Array` **rpc_get_recipients**\ (\ entity\: :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_get_recipients>`

Returns the peers an RPC on ``entity`` is sent to, which is every connected peer except this one. Offline, it returns ``[1]``. Use :ref:`interest_player_sees()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_player_sees>` to ask who can see the entity.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_request_call:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **rpc_request_call**\ (\ peer\: :godot:`int`, callable\: :godot:`Callable`, args\: :godot:`Array`, timeout_seconds\: :godot:`float` = 0.0\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_request_call>`

Calls ``callable`` on ``peer`` and returns a :ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` that resolves with its return value. The promise is rejected after ``timeout_seconds``, and at once with the reason when :ref:`rpc_call()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_call>` would refuse the call.

A ``timeout_seconds`` of ``0.0`` waits as long as the method's :ref:`NetwMemberConfig.timeout()<class_NetwMemberConfig_method_timeout>`, or 5 seconds when it declares none.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_request_call_group:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwGroupPromise<class_NetwGroupPromise>` **rpc_request_call_group**\ (\ callable\: :godot:`Callable`, args\: :godot:`Array`, timeout_seconds\: :godot:`float` = 0.0\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_request_call_group>`

Calls ``callable`` on every peer that sees its entity, and returns a :ref:`NetwGroupPromise<class_NetwGroupPromise>` of their replies. A peer that disconnects counts as rejected. The promise fails at once when :ref:`rpc_request_call()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_request_call>` would refuse the call.

\ ``timeout_seconds`` works as in :ref:`rpc_request_call()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_request_call>`. A ``call_local`` method also runs on this peer, as for :ref:`Netw.request_all()<class_Netw_method_request_all>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_declare:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **scene_declare**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_declare>`

Makes ``entity`` a multiplayer scene that replicates its nodes to the players in it. Call it before :ref:`entity_bind_node()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_bind_node>`. A client can only declare an entity that has a route from the server. :ref:`scene_undeclare()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_undeclare>` reverses it.

::

    var mirror := api.entity_create()
    api.entity_bind_route(mirror, route) # the server's route
    api.scene_declare(mirror)

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the entity is a scene
    ┠╴ERR_UNAUTHORIZED    called on a client without the entity's route
    ┠╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  the entity does not exist
    ┖╴ERR_UNCONFIGURED    the entity already has a node

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

Replicates ``scene`` to ``peer`` until :ref:`scene_unwatch()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_unwatch>`, without moving any of its player entities. Applies immediately.

    ┠╴ERR_UNAUTHORIZED       called on a client
    ┠╴ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER  peer is 0

Same as :godot:`SceneMultiplayer.send_auth() <SceneMultiplayer#class_SceneMultiplayer_method_send_auth>`. Returns :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNCONFIGURED <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNCONFIGURED>` when there is no :ref:`inner<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_inner>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_send_bytes:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **send_bytes**\ (\ bytes\: :godot:`PackedByteArray`, id\: :godot:`int` = 0, mode\: :godot:`MultiplayerPeer.TransferMode <MultiplayerPeer#enum_multiplayerpeer_TransferMode>` = 2, channel\: :godot:`int` = 0\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_send_bytes>`

Same as :godot:`SceneMultiplayer.send_bytes() <SceneMultiplayer#class_SceneMultiplayer_method_send_bytes>`. Returns :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNCONFIGURED <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNCONFIGURED>` when there is no :godot:`MultiplayerAPI.multiplayer_peer <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_property_multiplayer_peer>`. Networked reserves twelve first bytes, ``0x4E``, ``0x56``, ``0x57``, ``0x58``, ``0x6E``, ``0x76``, ``0x77``, ``0x78``, ``0x8E``, ``0x96``, ``0x97`` and ``0x98``. A packet that starts with one of them never reaches :ref:`peer_packet<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_packet>`, so give a raw packet a first byte of your own.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_send_to:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **send_to**\ (\ peer\: :godot:`int`, route\: :godot:`int`, channel\: :godot:`int`, payload\: :godot:`PackedByteArray`, reliable\: :godot:`bool`, comp\: :godot:`int` = 0, path\: :godot:`String` = "", batched\: :godot:`bool` = false\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_send_to>`

Sends ``payload`` on ``channel`` to ``peer``, about the entity at ``route``. A payload for this peer is handled locally. When a clock runs, some channels wait and send together at the next tick.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                     the payload was sent or queued
    ┠╴ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER  route is negative
    ┖╴ERR_UNCONFIGURED       the session cannot send yet

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_defer:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **session_defer**\ (\ fn\: :godot:`Callable`, key\: :godot:`StringName` = &""\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_defer>`

Queues ``fn`` to run at the next :ref:`session_flush_deferred()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_flush_deferred>`. A ``key`` already queued moves to the back and runs once. Calls queued while flushing run in the same flush.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_flush_deferred:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **session_flush_deferred**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_flush_deferred>`

Runs every call queued with :ref:`session_defer()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_defer>` until none are left. This is where the session reacts to nodes added, moved or freed. The session calls it every frame. Call it to see the result of tree changes before then.

Do not call it from :godot:`Node.tree_entered <Node#class_Node_signal_tree_entered>` or :godot:`Node.tree_exiting <Node#class_Node_signal_tree_exiting>`, since a :godot:`Node.reparent() <Node#class_Node_method_reparent>` may be half done. An error is pushed if calls keep queueing themselves forever.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_flush_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **session_flush_tick**\ (\ tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_flush_tick>`

Sends everything for ``tick``. The session calls it at the end of every tick. Call it only when driving ticks without the clock. Returns the error of :ref:`interest_flush_now()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_flush_now>` when that fails.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_leave:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **session_leave**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_leave>`

Disconnects and returns to :ref:`NetwSession.STATE_OFFLINE<class_NetwSession_constant_STATE_OFFLINE>`. On the server, it saves every persistent entity first, and a failed save keeps the session online. The promise resolves with an :godot:`@GlobalScope.Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` once the peer is closed.

::

    var left := api.session_leave()
    if not left.is_settled:
        await left.wait()
    if left.result != OK:
        push_error("still online: %s" % error_string(left.result))

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_prepare_join:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **session_prepare_join**\ (\ username\: :godot:`StringName`, args\: :godot:`Array` = []\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_prepare_join>`

Sets the join request sent when :godot:`MultiplayerAPI.multiplayer_peer <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_property_multiplayer_peer>` connects. The promise result is an :godot:`@GlobalScope.Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>`.

::

    var prepared := api.session_prepare_join(&"Dev")
    if not prepared.is_settled:
        await prepared.wait()
    if int(prepared.result) != OK:
        return

\ An empty ``username`` returns :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER>`.

\ **Player request.**

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_save:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **table_save**\ (\ table\: :godot:`RID`, database\: :godot:`RID`, key\: :godot:`StringName`, ids\: :godot:`PackedStringArray`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_save>`

Saves the rows of ``table`` under ``key`` in ``database``. ``ids`` names each row, in the order of :ref:`table_read_routes()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_read_routes>`. See :ref:`NetwTable.save()<class_NetwTable_method_save>` for the errors.

\ **Server Only.**

Writes ``data`` to ``column`` of ``table``. It applies at :ref:`table_commit()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_commit>`.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the column was written
    ┠╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  the table does not exist
    ┠╴ERR_UNCONFIGURED    the schema is not sealed
    ┖╴ERR_INVALID_DATA    unknown column, or data has the wrong type

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_cancel_peer_creation:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **transport_cancel_peer_creation**\ (\ ticket\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_cancel_peer_creation>`

Cancels the :ref:`transport_create_peer()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_create_peer>` request ``ticket``. Its callback receives ``(null, ERR_SKIP, "")``. Does nothing once the callback has started.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_create_peer:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **transport_create_peer**\ (\ transport\: :godot:`RID`, mode\: :ref:`TransportMode<enum_NetwConnection_TransportMode>`, address\: :godot:`String`, settings\: :godot:`Dictionary`, completed\: :godot:`Callable`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_create_peer>`

Asks ``transport`` to create a :godot:`MultiplayerPeer` and returns a ticket for :ref:`transport_cancel_peer_creation()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_cancel_peer_creation>`. ``address`` is used by clients and ``settings`` by hosts.

\ ``completed`` is called once, in a later frame, as ``completed(peer, error, detail)``. Assign the peer to :godot:`MultiplayerAPI.multiplayer_peer <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_property_multiplayer_peer>` inside this callback, before any ``await``. Returning without assigning it closes the peer.

Returns an invalid :godot:`RID` when ``completed`` is not valid.

::

    var ticket := api.transport_create_peer(
            relay, NetwConnection.TRANSPORT_MODE_CLIENT,
            "eu-1.example.com", {}, _created)

    func _created(peer: MultiplayerPeer, error: Error, _detail: String):
        if error != OK:
            return
        api.multiplayer_peer = peer

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_find:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **transport_find**\ (\ peer_class\: :godot:`StringName`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_find>`

Returns the transport that creates ``peer_class`` peers, or an invalid :godot:`RID`.

::

    var enet := api.transport_find(&"ENetMultiplayerPeer")
    api.transport_host(enet, {port = 21253})

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_get_param:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Variant` **transport_get_param**\ (\ transport\: :godot:`RID`, param\: :ref:`TransportParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportParam>`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_get_param>`

Returns one property of ``transport``, used to draw host and join forms, or ``null``. :ref:`TRANSPORT_PARAM_CAPABILITIES<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_PARAM_CAPABILITIES>` returns a :ref:`TransportCapability<enum_NetwConnection_TransportCapability>` mask.

::

    var caps: int = api.transport_get_param(
            t, NetwMultiplayer.TRANSPORT_PARAM_CAPABILITIES)
    if caps & NetwConnection.TRANSPORT_CAN_HOST:
        show_host_button()

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_register:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **transport_register**\ (\ type\: :godot:`Script`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_register>`

Registers the :ref:`NetwTransport<class_NetwTransport>` script ``type`` with this session. Registering it again returns the same :godot:`RID`. Returns an invalid :godot:`RID` when another script is already registered for the same peer class. It replaces the built-in transport of that class.

::

    func _ready() -> void:
        var api := Netw.of(self)
        var relay := api.transport_register(MyRelayTransport)
        var caps: int = api.transport_get_param(
                relay, NetwMultiplayer.TRANSPORT_PARAM_CAPABILITIES)

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_set_defaults:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **transport_set_defaults**\ (\ transport\: :godot:`RID`, settings\: :godot:`Dictionary`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_set_defaults>`

The flat form of :ref:`NetwConnection.set_defaults()<class_NetwConnection_method_set_defaults>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_unregister:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **transport_unregister**\ (\ transport\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_unregister>`

Reverses :ref:`transport_register()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_register>`. A peer it already created stays connected. Pending :ref:`transport_create_peer()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_create_peer>` requests fail with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNAVAILABLE <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNAVAILABLE>`.

::

    var relay := api.transport_register(MyRelayTransport)
    # ... later, when the relay SDK is shut down
    api.transport_unregister(relay)

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the transport was removed
    ┖╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  not registered on this session
